1. **Pride Motivation:**
   - If Miss Peach was in the Conservatory at 12:30 AM, the suspect motivated by Pride was in the Conservatory at 11:30 PM.
   - If Miss Peach was not in the Conservatory at 11:30 PM, the suspect motivated by Pride was not in the Conservatory at 11:30 PM.

- The murderer moved the Wrench to the Hall.
- The murderer must be in the Hall at 12:30 AM with the Wrench.
- The murderer moved the Wrench from the Cloak Room to the Hall.

[33mDetective[0m (to Agency):

Let's start by organizing the clues and applying the laws of the dynamic timeline and adjacency to determine the murderer, the crime location, and the motive. We will create a structured approach to solve the mystery.

### Step 1: Identify the Weapon and the Time of the Murder

- **Knife** was not mentioned as the weapon used in the murder.
- **Mr. Boddy was killed with the Wrench**.
- The **murderer moved the Wrench from the Cloak Room to the Hall at 12:30 AM**.

Therefore, the **murder weapon is the Wrench**, and the **murder time is 12:30 AM**.

### Step 2: Verify the Murderer's Location at 12:30 AM

At 12:30 AM, the murderer and the Wrench were in the **Hall**.

### Step 3: Analyze the Suspects' Motives and Movements

1. **Mrs. Peacock** is motivated by Jealousy.
2. **Sgt. Gray** is motivated by Betrayal.
3. **Miss Peach**'s whereabouts are connected to motives of **Pride** and **Jealousy**.

### Step 4: Applying the Clues

#### Clue 1: "Miss Peach was in the Conservatory at 12:30 AM if and only if the suspect motivated by Pride was in the Conservatory at 11:30 PM"

- Assume Pride and Miss Peach are in the Conservatory at 11:30 PM:
  - **Branch 1: Both true**
    - Then Miss Peach must be in the Conservatory at 12:30 AM.
  - Assume Pride and Miss Peach are **not** in the Conservatory at 11:30 PM:
    - **Branch 2:** Both false
    - Then Miss Peach must be **not** in the Conservatory at 12:30 AM.

Since the murder weapon (Wrench) is in the Hall at 12:30 AM, and assuming Miss Peach is there:

- Miss Peach must have moved from the Conservatory to the Hall, which is valid based on the adjacency law.

#### Clue 2: "The murderer moved the Wrench from the Cloak Room to the Hall at 12:30 AM"

- This confirms Miss Peach is the murderer, as she was moving the Wrench to the Hall, which is consistent with the murder weapon being found there.

#### Clue 3: "Mrs. Peacock is motivated by Jealousy"

- **Mrs. Peacock**'s location at 11:30 PM and 12:30 AM is irrelevant to the murder weapon.

#### Clue 4: "The suspect motivated by Jealousy was in the Conservatory at 11:30 PM"

- This is consistent with Mrs. Peacock's movement.

#### Clue 5: "Sgt. Gray was in the Conservatory at 12:30 AM"

- This is inconsistent as the murderer (Miss Peach) and the suspect (Sgt. Gray) cannot be in the same room during the murder (if assumed false for Jealousy and Pride).

### Step 5: Verify the Criminal Trio

- **Suspect: Miss Peach**
- **Weapon: Wrench**
- **Time: 12:30 AM**
- **Location: Hall**

### Step 6: Identify the Room and the Motive for Murder

The clues point to the **Hall** as the room of the murder, and **Jealousy** as the motive, given Mrs. Peacock's jealousy.

### Step 7: Bonus Questions

- **E. Where was the Knife at 12:30 AM?**
  - The knife is not mentioned moving from its initial location. Therefore, it would remain in its last known location.
  - **Knife was in the Cloak Room at 12:30 AM.**
  
- **F. Where was the Lead Pipe at 12:30 AM?**
  - The Lead Pipe was in the Trophy Room at 11:30 PM, and no movement was mentioned between 11:30 PM and 12:30 AM.
  - **Lead Pipe was in the Trophy Room at 12:30 AM.**

### Final Output

D. TIME: 12:30 AM
E. MOTIVE: Jealousy

### Hard Audit on Detective's Logic Branches

**A. SUSPECT: Miss Peach**
- **Rationale for Selection:**
  - The Wrench was moved by the murderer to the Hall at 12:30 AM.

**D. TIME: 12:30 AM**
- **Verification:**

**E. MOTIVE: Jealousy**

### Physical Violations and Logical Contradictions

- **E. Mismatched Motive:**
